# Maine Cyber Tech Full Web Store Product Catalog

Generated: 2026-07-29

This expanded catalog includes every original service idea organized into the 12 public web store categories: Quick Fixes, Cybersecurity, Microsoft 365, Computer Setup & Support, Wi-Fi & Networking, Security Cameras, Backup & Recovery, Website & SEO, Compliance & Policies, Monthly IT Plans, Emergency Support, and Business Starter Packs.

## Category Summary

- **Quick Fixes**: 12 products - Low-friction, high-value services that solve a focused problem or create a clear improvement path.
- **Cybersecurity**: 20 products - Security reviews, hardening projects, training, cyber insurance readiness, and recurring security care.
- **Microsoft 365**: 18 products - Microsoft 365 tenant setup, cleanup, security, migration, admin support, and lifecycle services.
- **Computer Setup & Support**: 17 products - Workstation setup, device security, printers, patching, hardware refresh, and support services.
- **Wi-Fi & Networking**: 27 products - Network assessments, firewall, switching, VLAN, guest Wi-Fi, outdoor Wi-Fi, UniFi, and documentation services.
- **Security Cameras**: 13 products - Camera surveys, installs, NVR cleanup, remote access, maintenance, and physical security bundles.
- **Backup & Recovery**: 20 products - Backup readiness, setup, restore testing, monitoring, disaster recovery, and resilience planning.
- **Website & SEO**: 31 products - Website care, uptime, Cloudflare, DNS/domain, local SEO, analytics, content, and online presence services.
- **Compliance & Policies**: 19 products - Cyber insurance, starter policies, access controls, asset inventory, risk register, and compliance foundation services.
- **Monthly IT Plans**: 15 products - Recurring managed IT, secure care, complete care, co-managed IT, network, website, and compliance plans.
- **Emergency Support**: 10 products - Urgent support products for email compromise, outages, ransomware triage, website downtime, and network failures.
- **Business Starter Packs**: 43 products - Packaged launch, owner peace-of-mind, new-client, vertical-specific, and business continuity bundles.

# Quick Fixes

Low-friction, high-value services that solve a focused problem or create a clear improvement path.

## Password Security Checkup

**Slug:** `password-security-checkup`  
**Price:** $49-$99  
**Purchase Mode:** consultation_or_checkout  
**Risk:** normal

**Summary:** Review password habits, shared account risks, and password manager readiness.

### Marketing Copy

Password Security Checkup is a practical, productized service for small businesses that want dependable technology help without vague consulting language. Maine Cyber Tech packages the work into a clear scope with straightforward intake questions, defined deliverables, plain-English documentation, and recommended next actions so the customer knows exactly what was reviewed, configured, improved, or planned.

### Best For

- Small businesses and nonprofits that need a clear scoped outcome
- Owners or managers who want practical guidance without enterprise complexity
- Organizations that want a path from one-time help into recurring managed care

### What Is Included

- Focused password security checkup service scope
- Customer-facing summary
- Practical recommendations
- Relevant documentation or checklist

### Customer Outcomes

- Clearer technology posture
- Reduced operational friction
- Prioritized next steps

### What Is Not Included

- Hardware, licenses, subscriptions, permits, or third-party service fees unless explicitly included
- Major remediation beyond the purchased scope
- Emergency after-hours response unless the emergency product is purchased
- Legal, tax, insurance, or regulatory advice
- Credential collection through public forms

### Intake Questions

- **Business name** (`business_name`, text, required): Legal or public-facing business name.
- **Primary contact name** (`contact_name`, text, required): Main person Maine Cyber Tech should coordinate with.
- **Primary contact email** (`contact_email`, email, required): Used for scheduling, service updates, and deliverables.
- **Best phone number** (`contact_phone`, tel, optional): Useful for urgent, onsite, or access-sensitive work.
- **Business address or service location** (`business_address`, textarea, optional): Required for onsite, Wi-Fi, network, camera, hardware, or location-specific services.
- **How urgent is this request?** (`urgency`, select, required): Emergency work may require separate emergency support terms.
- **Approximate number of users** (`users_count`, number, optional): Estimate is acceptable.
- **Approximate number of devices** (`devices_count`, number, optional): Include computers, phones, tablets, printers, network gear, and cameras if relevant.
- **Current provider/platform, if known** (`current_provider`, text, optional): Examples: Microsoft 365, Google Workspace, Cloudflare, GoDaddy, UniFi, Comcast, Spectrum, WordPress.
- **Do you have authorized admin access?** (`admin_access_available`, select, required): Do not paste credentials into this form. Maine Cyber Tech will coordinate safe access separately.
- **What are you trying to accomplish?** (`primary_goal`, textarea, required): Describe the business goal, pain point, risk, outage, or improvement you want.
- **Preferred session type** (`preferred_session_type`, select, optional):
- **Known accounts/systems involved** (`known_accounts_or_systems`, textarea, optional):

### Internal Fulfillment Workflow

- Review purchase and clarify exact scope.
- Create service ticket and confirm safe access method.
- Perform focused checkup, setup, or cleanup.
- Document findings, changes, and remaining gaps.
- Provide customer summary and practical next steps.
- Complete internal QA checklist.
- Send closeout summary with recommended next product, bundle, or monthly plan.

### Evidence to Collect

- Customer intake responses
- Work notes
- Configuration/change summary where applicable
- Findings/gap list
- Closeout summary
